## THE VACUITY FLOOR AND THE DE-ESCALATION CLAUSE (flagship adjudication, 2026-08-21)

**"Weak earns its own round; vacuous does not get to ship" IS A RATIONALISATION UNTIL A FLOOR IS
ATTACHED** �?" the adjudicating seat said so about the main loop's own call, and it was right:
*weak* has no lower bound, so any detector that kills one mutant can claim it.

> **THE FLOOR.** A detector is VACUOUS �?" replace it IN-ROUND �?" unless it (a) fails on the
> pre-fix build, (b) fails on a **VERBATIM REINSTATEMENT of the removed defect at EVERY fixed
> site**, and (c) fails on a null medium (a stale copy, a comment-only file �?" i.e. it carries
> identity rows). Below the floor, the round's own claim to have "shipped a detector with the
> fix" is a WRONG RECORD, which the stopping rule already fixes in-round.
>
> ### *** CLAUSE (d) �?" NULL-REPAIR RED. Adopted 2026-08-23 by OWNER RULING, on the round
> that measured its case. ***
>
> **(d) The detector must also fail when the fix is PRESENT but its REPAIR IS NULLED �?"
> the guard kept, its effect removed.**
>
> **(b) DOES NOT IMPLY (d), and #461 measured both in the same hour on the same detector:**
>
> | mutant | shape | score |
> |---|---|---|
> | `m_dmarevert` | (b): reinstate the `exit(1)` verbatim | **22/31 �?" killed** |
> | `m_align1` | (d): keep the guard, weaken `& 3` to `& 1` | **27/27 �?" ESCAPED** |
>
> The detector passed (a), (b) and (c) and was still blind to a ONE-CHARACTER edit that
> re-opened the very defect the round had just closed. (b) restores the DEFECT; (d) neuters
> the FIX. A detector can be sensitive to one and blind to the other, because they fail at
> different sites: (b) fails where the old code was, (d) fails where the new code is.
>
> **HOW TO BUILD THE (d) MUTANT �?" and it is the same discriminator the accept-row rule
> already uses.** Take each predicate the fix **DEPENDS ON** and weaken it to the
> least-restrictive form that still *reads* like the same check: a mask narrowed
> (`& 3` -> `& 1`), a condition qualified (`if (X)` -> `if (X && writeflag == MEM_WRITE)`),
> a bound relaxed. **Not deletion** �?" deletion is (b) wearing (d)'s clothes and is usually
> already covered. The test is whether a plausible future edit could produce it.
>
> **WHY THE ONE-CHARACTER FORM IS THE ONE THAT MATTERS.** `dma alias`, the row written
> specifically to pin that repair, was **STRUCTURALLY BLIND** to it: it used an ODD address,
> which both guard forms drop. *The row aimed at the defect could not see the defect return.*
> A row written from the FIX's intent tests the intent, not the boundary �?" so (d)'s mutant
> has to come from an adversary, which is exactly what the ask-again buys.
>
> **COST: one mutant per repair predicate**, the same order as the accept-row rule (#448 = 4,
> #449 = 1, #461 = 4 across two arms). It is bounded by the diff, so it cannot become #392:
> the predicates are the ones the fix ADDED, and that list is finite and visible.
>
> ### *** "THE PREDICATE THE FIX INTRODUCED" ENUMERATED THE EMPTY SET FOR MOST OF THIS
> ### QUEUE, AND NOBODY NOTICED FOR THREE ROUNDS. Corrected 2026-08-24 -- clause (d) now
> ### reads DEPENDS ON, not INTRODUCED. ***
>
> Clause (d) says *keep the guard, null its repair*. As written it enumerated only
> predicates the diff ADDS -- and **the two commonest fix shapes here add none**:
>
> * a **pure deletion** introduces no predicate at all;
> * a **DE-FATALISATION** introduces none either -- it changes what an EXISTING
>   predicate's branch does. That is most of this project's queue.
>
> So for those rounds clause (d) enumerated **nothing**, and a round could satisfy the
> floor while having built no (d) mutant whatever. `#461` measured (d) successfully only
> because its fix happened to ADD a predicate (an alignment mask) for the mutant to weaken.
>
> **Reading it as DEPENDS ON fixes both cases at once**, and it is why this now replaces a
> narrower deletion-only rule written earlier the same day: that rule was a special case of
> this gap, and two overlapping statements of one rule is the accumulation this carrier is
> supposed to refuse.
>
> **The two shapes, once the enumeration is right:**
> * **fix ADDS a predicate** -> weaken the added one (unchanged; the original rule).
> * **fix adds none** -> the (d) mutant reroutes an EXISTING guard's repair. For a
>   de-fatalisation: **swap the guard's operand for a field the guarded BODY reads.**
>   For a pure deletion: **re-introduce the removed behaviour under a DIFFERENT predicate**
>   -- not the same one widened, which is (b) wearing (d)'s clothes.
>
> *** THE MEASURED CASE, and it is why this matters rather than being tidy: ***
> `#462` de-fatalised two arms. Its guard reads `d->data`; the body it guards reads
> `d->data` AND `d->data_len`; the two are set together in one place and cleared in two
> places that touch only ONE of them. Swapping the guard's operand to `d->data_len == 0`
> **scored a CLEAN SWEEP on a 52-row detector that had just passed twelve mutants**, and
> restored a NULL-pointer read of 408 bytes. Under the old wording that was coverage debt;
> under DEPENDS ON it is a **floor breach, L2, fix in-round** -- which is what the round
> did, before the wording was corrected.
>
> #### *** THE BOUND, and it is not optional: "DEPENDS ON" INVITES TRANSITIVE DATA-FLOW
> #### CHASING, which is unbounded and is how this becomes `#392`. Named 2026-08-24 by the
> #### adjudicating seat, in the same breath as upholding the correction. ***
>
> > **DEPENDS ON enumerates exactly two things: (1) the predicates DOMINATING the diff's
> > edited lines, walked up only to the ENCLOSING FUNCTION BOUNDARY -- never into callees
> > or callers; and (2) for operand-swap forms, swaps drawn ONLY from fields the guarded
> > BODY reads (the guard-field/body-field grep the pre-table check already mandates).**
>
> That is finite, derivable from the diff by reading, and **it provably contains the
> measured case**: the `d->data == NULL` guard dominates the edited decline (clause 1), and
> `data_len` is a body-read field (clause 2), so `M11` falls out mechanically rather than
> by inspiration. It cannot become `#392` because the enumeration stays closed over the
> diff's own hunks -- the same closure the INTRODUCED wording had, without the empty-set
> defect that made INTRODUCED enumerate nothing for a de-fatalisation.
>
> #### *** JOINT CELLS -- adjudicated 2026-09-04 on `pcc2widthkills` pass 10 by the flagship seat, after
> #### a measuring seat's CONJUNCTION of two body-read counts escaped 190/190 AND THE SAME SEAT ARGUED IT
> #### WAS NOT A BREACH.  Both proposed bounds were rejected; the one adopted came from neither. ***
>
> > **DEPENDS-ON enumerates JOINT cells over the guard's operands and the body-read lvalues EXACTLY
> > WHEN THE GUARDED BODY'S OWN CODE DISTINGUISHES THEM.**  For each lvalue take the PARTITION the
> > body's own tests induce (`pending > 0` gives {0, >0}; `relative_addr == PCCTWO_T1ICR` gives
> > {T1, T2}); the enumerated cells are the CROSS-PRODUCT of those partitions -- never arbitrary
> > boolean functions of the read-set.  A mutant exempting a single cell whose reachability is
> > MEASURED is a floor breach; close the whole cross-product in ONE diff and write into the
> > contract which cells are rowed.  A value or combination the body never tests as a case
> > (`idata == k`; a byte no conditional distinguishes; both counts >= 2 where the body tests `> 0`)
> > is DISCLOSED under clause (e).
>
> **Why this form and not the two that were argued.**  The dissent's discriminator -- *a plausible
> future edit* -- failed three ways: plausibility is not derivable by reading; the mutant was written
> and measured, so it is an edit someone writes; and "single-operand is complete" is measurably
> false, since `&& idata != 2` is single-operand and escapes.  The main loop's own bound -- *pairs of
> body-read lvalues over a finite reachable space* -- was rejected as SELF-REFUTING: the counts' raw
> value space is 2^32 apiece, so "finite" does no bounding work on its motivating case (a C `int` is
> finite -- the first wording here said it was not; codex and glm caught it on pass 11); "reachable
> guest state" would force rowing
> `idata == 2` that the same record discloses; and a PAIRS cap misses `#499`'s four axes.  The
> body-tested-partition form is finite (a product of small partition counts), derivable from the
> diff by reading the body's own `if`s, and it enumerates `#499`'s width x component x direction x
> position cells from `len`, `writeflag` and `relative_addr` -- which neither argued bound reached.
>
> **The measured case.**  The TxICR body tests both pending counts at `> 0` and the label at
> `== T1ICR` / `T2ICR`, so the closure is {T2, T1} x {p1 = 0, > 0} x {p2 = 0, > 0} -- eight cells.
> Pass 9 held one open cell (both counts, T2 label: a 190/190 escape, predicted by the Opus seat
> before its build); pass 10 found the MIRROR (both counts, T1 label) and the (p2 only, T1 label)
> cell open -- the `#499` shape reproduced inside the repair for it -- and the label x p1 x p2 x IEN
> product (sixteen cells) closed in one diff of nine readbacks in existing sessions; the ICLR, INT and
> 0xe0 partitions were disclosed by count, and pass 11 then found the ICLR x pending cell open and
> in-rule (this paragraph said "eight cells ... three readbacks" until pass 11).  **The De Morgan form of the gap**, from the kimi
> substitute: `!(p1 && p2)` is `p1 == 0 || p2 == 0`; neither disjunct is true of all its lvalue's
> held values, the disjunction is true in every row, so a per-lvalue disclosure test can never fire
> on it.  That is why the per-lvalue value-domain statement, correct as far as it went, could not
> see this class at all.
>
> **Two corollaries from the same pass, both measured.**  The PRE-TABLE CHECK's SIBLING PARITY row
> compares BLOCKS; at a block that serves TWO case labels the siblings are the LABELS, and a
> parity census over the row names is what would have shown `pendboth` as the only T2-only cell
> before its table ran (Opus, pass 10).  **AND THE JOIN ITSELF, adjudicated 2026-09-09 on `sgimtedepth4dead` 7g-1 after a MEASURED total escape: a dispatch LABEL is a floor axis of an edited guard when the selector's value FLOWS INTO an lvalue the guarded body reads or writes within the enclosing function (there, `dst_mode = (dst_tlb << 10) + ...` two lines above a repair of `dst_mode |= RGBA`), or when the body re-tests it after the join (TxICR); a label that reaches the edit and touches nothing the body reads is clause (e) (the `mode & COPY` shape).  A detector rowing one label of three was swept 14/14 by `&& dst_tlb == MTE_TLB_A` while a guest-only control showed the closed defect live at the other two (`regress/artifacts/sgimtedepth4dead/killtable3_20260909/`).  The floor it adds is {labels} x the guard operand's body-tested partition -- six cells there, two rows owed -- never the labels' cross-product with everything.**  **AND A GUARD THAT SUPPRESSES A DISPATCH ADDS A THIRD DISJUNCT TO THE 2026-09-09 LABEL CLAUSE, adjudicated 2026-09-25 on `#529` by the flagship's first-hand Q3 re-ruling, with GPT-6 Astra's amendments of 2026-09-26 (`crimewidthkill/rerule_20260925/`); this text is the codex seat's 2026-09-26 rewrite of the clause (`codexfarm_20260926/codex_carrierq3.txt`, "faithful with changes"), adopted with three corrections by the flagship's final ruling of 2026-09-30 (`crimewidthkill/final529r18_20260926/fable5_final.md`, item E): a dispatch LABEL is ALSO a floor axis when the edited guard's repair bypasses that dispatch in the enclosing function.  This is an alternative to selector data flow into the guarded body or re-testing after the join; absence of those two connections does not put a qualifying suppression label in clause (e).  At CRIME the repair is `goto crime_declined`, bypassing the register switch.  The jump omits the dispatch; that omission does not make the whole repair path empty of reads, writes or tests (it reads and writes the latch).  The added axis consists of the skipped dispatch's GUEST-OBSERVABLE labels.  Cross it with the applicable code-tested predicate and state partitions under the existing bounds: the guard's dominating predicates and the jointly reachable device-state lvalues read by the fix's own repair path, as defined by pass 11 above.  This does not make every test or state in the skipped arms a floor axis; the existing partition, reachability and generating-set bounds still apply.  At `#529` the repair path tests the warning latch, so its CLEAR and SET states belong explicitly in the enumeration.  TIME and REV with the latch SET were the demonstrated holes at repair 14 -- `M3_timelatch` and `M5_revlatch` escaped at 204/204 while `M4_timeplain` was detected at 200/204 and `M6_istlatch` at 201/204 (`repair14_20260916/POSTTABLE.out`) -- and were CLOSED by repair 15 (table 14: 214/218 both; table 16: 222/226 both, `table16_20260926/POSTTABLE16.out`).  Those holes were never the whole enumeration, and detection of the plain TIME variant does not discharge the latch-conditioned variants.  The INTMASK and CONTROL-write label-swaps were measured DETECTED: table 13a `M7_intmasklatch` / `M8_ctllatch` 201/204 each, table 16 223/226 each.  An OFFSET within one label is not an axis unless the body distinguishes it: `#529`'s entry 34 (`sd +0x40`, latch CLEAR) is unrowed while its label cell is held at +0x278 -- the unrowed-slot class, clause (e).  Include only labels whose skipped arms have guest-observable effects.  CRIME's DOG arm is a bare `break;` and its `default:` body is host-debug only, so neither contributes a floor label for THIS skipped-dispatch face.  This exclusion does not establish guest equivalence for ACCEPTED accesses at those labels: CRIME's width arm supplies `+0x0c`'s observable value before `default:` prints.  DISCHARGE requires measured DETECTION by the shipped instrument of each enumerated label-swap variant, including the relevant state-conditioned variants.  Existing rows may discharge them; predictions or aggregate detection do not substitute for the individual measurements.  Table 11's K2 PER-ROW prediction error is the recorded caution.  The added label enumeration is bounded to the ONE skipped dispatch in the enclosing function; it does not recurse into further dispatches or dispatches in callees.  MINTED FROM ONE DEVICE: on first reuse, check that the skipped-label enumeration and its bounded predicate/state product fit the new device.**  And the measuring seat's own bound -- the cross-product
> of the value-CLASSES the rows establish, with the dispatch axes as full axes, demand
> INCREMENTAL -- agrees with the body-tested form on every cell that mattered here and differs on
> where the classes come from (the rows, or the body's tests); that residual difference is
> carried to the next pass as a named question rather than settled by the author.
>
> #### *** THE LETTER OVER-DEMANDED, AND ITS OWN AUTHOR BOUNDED IT ONE PASS LATER (pass 11, 2026-09-04). ***
>
> Read literally, "the cross-product of the body-tested partitions" demanded ALL 128 cells at the TxICR
> arm -- the body also tests `idata & IEN`, `idata & ICLR`, the register's INT bit and `idata & 0xe0` --
> and it compounds as 2^k in the `if`-count across twelve arms: the `#392` shape wearing the rule's
> clothes.  Three seats said the letter demands the remainder; the flagship seat, asked to be harder on
> its own rule than on anyone, ruled AGAINST the letter:
>
> > **The rule ENUMERATES joint cells wherever the body distinguishes them; the FLOOR -- the cells a row
> > is OWED for -- is the cross-product of the guard's dominating predicates with the device-STATE lvalues
> > the fix's own repair path reads that are JOINTLY REACHABLE through its own data flow.**  The bits of
> > the single value being written are single-operand MARGINALS, not floor; joint cells outside the floor
> > are clause-(e) coverage debt, subject to the one ask-again.  A filter may reject or discharge a cell
> > but may demand a new row only for a floor cell -- so it cannot grow unbounded.
>
> At TxICR the floor is {label} x {p1} x {p2}: one `pcc_timer_tick` callback increments both counts and
> `reassert_interrupts` reads both, so the pair is a joint input of the model.  Eight cells, all rowed.
>
> #### *** THE FLOOR INCLUDES THE SINGLE-AXIS GENERATING SET, AND "DERIVED" STATE IS NOT DISPOSABLE.
> #### Adjudicated 2026-09-04 by the fix review (Fable 5.1 adjudicating; GPT-6 Astra at max dissenting on the
> #### bound and supplying the two findings that made the amendment measurable). ***
>
> > **At each floor cell, every idata bit the guarded body TESTS is written both clear and set.**  The bare
> > state floor above does not catch `M_ICLR` -- an idata-bit x state joint -- and would grade the pass-11
> > escape as debt while the CHANGELOG grades it L2; the generating set is what makes the two consistent.
> > Multi-axis idata joints stay clause-(e) debt.  **The codex seat adopts the Opus strength-t covering
> > array instead** (its `t` = the body's longest conjunction) and is RECORDED, not adopted -- for COST (seven
> > binary axes at TxICR make a strength-4 array tens of pty cells per arm: the 2^k growth by another door) and
> > because a covering array certifies that a tuple OCCURS, not that an observable differs, so it cannot be the
> > floor without the CHANNEL obligation doing the work.  *This clause first said "`t` is a judgment input";
> > the array's own author refuted that on pass 13 -- `t` is read off the body -- and supplied the two grounds.*
>
> > #### *** "TESTS" MEANS **MASKS OR COMPARES AS A CASE**, NOT "DEPENDS ON".  Pinned 2026-09-25 by the flagship
> > #### ruling on `#549` v6's adjudication pass (D1), after ONE true quotation of the sentence above carried TWO
> > #### readings through two passes. ***
> >
> > `idata & ICLR` tests a bit.  **A RELATIONAL compare such as `idata >= 8` tests NO bit: its generating set is its
> > three regions, below / at / past the bound** -- `#548`'s P_win ruling -- so at each floor cell the obligation is
> > those three regions, never "every bit set alone".  Crossing a floor cell with a single bit of a compared value is
> > the two-axis joint a probe's NOT CLAIMED (0) discloses; `#549`'s latch x bit was graded that way even though its
> > measured consequence above bit 32 was the round's own defect (silent truncation onto a legal port), because
> > grading by consequence would reopen `#548` (`pass_v6/fable5_f2_v6.md`).  **Four packet-fed seats read the
> > sentence without its bounds -- my brief quoted it alone -- and all four graded the joint FLOOR:** a rule's
> > bounds travel with its quotation, or the quotation is a different rule.
>
> **The two measured consequences, both found by a seat reading the ENUMERATION against the handler rather
> than the tables against the enumeration:**
>
> * **The enumeration listed five arms as having NO `if` and each has one** -- VECBASE, SCCICR, SCCTX, SCCRX,
>   SCSIICR test their HUH? bits on the composed idata.  The record then said every wide value the rows drove
>   at them had those bits SET, so `&& (idata & ~mask)` would sweep the table.  **Measured FALSE the same
>   evening (part 13, M_HUHV 249/251):** two announce-count rows (`latch all zero`, `latch all one`) already
>   drove HUH?-clear values (0x0000 alone at VECBASE, whose mask is 0xf0; the six `latch all*` rows at the
>   0x1f/0xdf arms -- 244/251 there), and their counts drop when the write lands -- the partition was HELD by a channel
>   the enumeration did not know it had.  *An enumeration is checked against the BODY and against the ROWS
>   before a breach is declared; "it would sweep the table" is a MEASUREMENT, never an inference -- and the
>   seat that raised the finding had said so ("existing rows may discharge their partitions; the enumeration
>   must actually show that") while the main loop wrote the sweep as a fact.*
> * **The INT bit was dismissed as "derived from the count" and it is not**: `reassert_interrupts()` only SETS
>   it, the body clears it only on ICLR, and an IEN-clear byte write zeroes the count -- so (INT set, count 0)
>   is a reachable joint state of two body-read lvalues that no row held.  **Reachability of a joint cell is
>   derived from the WRITERS of each lvalue, never from a steady-state invariant** -- "INT follows the count"
>   was true between ticks and false across the one write the guest driver makes to disable a timer.
>
> *Both went to pass 13 as floor cells (nine rows, seven mutants).  The fix itself was unchanged by the review,
> which is the finding worth keeping about the review: eleven passes on the detector and none on the fix, and
> the first pass on the fix approved it block by block while finding the detector's enumeration wrong.*
>
> **What the debt costs when it has a measured warrant, and it is cheap:** the measuring seat built the
> disclosed `&& !(p2 && (idata & ICLR))` and it escaped the 203-row instrument, as three reading seats
> had predicted -- so it was repaired in the GENERATING-SET form (grok), and GRADED **L2**, a floor breach
> repaired in-round, by the pass-11 disposition in the CHANGELOG (the cell was inside the closure by the
> round's own pass-3 sentence).  *This clause said "L1 construction" until 2026-09-04, when the GPT-6 Astra
> fix review found the carrier and the CHANGELOG grading ONE finding two ways; the CHANGELOG's is the ruling
> and this is the correction.*  The form: at every
> rowed pending cell, write each tested idata bit both clear and set, so any single-axis extra conjunct on
> one of them lands a write and reds a readback.  Twelve wide writes appended to three existing sessions;
> no new session, no new load sensitivity.  Multi-axis idata joints stay disclosed; INT-clear-with-a-count-
> pending is disclosed as UNREACHABLE with its derivation (the tick increments only under IEN and reassert
> sets INT before any guard runs), which is the honest form of a cell nobody rows.
>
> *Two records lessons from the same pass.  A CORRECTION IS NOT SAFER THAN THE THING IT CORRECTS: pass 10
> accepted "the store mask is a writer of ICLR" and made a true sentence false -- `0x17 & 0x08 == 0`, one
> AND that nobody ran.  And a PREDICTION COPIED INTO A MEASURED COLUMN is the frozen-number class in a new
> coat: a table said (0x13) where the log said 0x33; the score matched by coincidence and the value was a
> wrong record until a seat read the log.*
>
> *Count the arguments, not the seats: seven of eight landed seats put the pair inside the closure,
> and the one that did not (minimax) was reading the CONTRACT's per-lvalue wording rather than the
> clause -- the wording the discriminator sentence now replaces.*

> ### *** THE PRE-TABLE CHECK -- three greps, ~30 min, and it protects the ASK-AGAIN'S
> ### CAP rather than the clock. Adopted 2026-08-24; two seats derived it independently. ***
>
> > ## *** MANDATORY as of 2026-08-28, by flagship adjudication. It was RECOMMENDED prose for
> > ## four days and TWO rounds skipped it -- which is this file's own structure-first trigger,
> > ## met exactly. ***
> >
> > **SCOPE, and this is what answers the cost objection: it binds EXACTLY the population the
> > vacuity floor already binds** -- any round shipping or EXTENDING a kill-table-backed
> > detector. **Not every round.** Records-only, documented-only and table-less harness rounds
> > are untouched.
> >
> > **FORM: recorded OUTPUT, never assertion** -- the two-part-clearance precedent, *run it, do
> > not assert it*. One `PRETABLE.out` in the round's kill-table artifacts dir (or a probe-header
> > section), **written BEFORE table 1 runs so its own mtime proves the order** -- the same
> > order discriminator the oracle-correction rule uses. It carries: the sibling arms compared
> > and any asymmetry; each enumerated claim sentence mapped to a row or an owed sentence; the
> > guard-field/body-field lists and any disagreeing-state row owed; and per row, the mutant it
> > kills and the observable that differs.
> >
> > **Any check may discharge `N/A` WITH ITS REASON RECORDED. An unrun check and an inapplicable
> > one must not render the same** -- this project's oldest rule about blanks.
> >
> > ### *** AND A THIRD FAILURE MODE, MEASURED 2026-08-28: THE BLOCK WAS RUN, ITS ROWS WERE
> > ### PRESCRIBED, AND THEY WERE LOST WHEN THE ROW LIST WAS REWRITTEN THREE HOURS LATER. ***
> >
> > `#490`'s `PRETABLE.out` named two mutants at **16:05** -- a latch-bit collision and a brace
> > slip -- and prescribed a row for each (`crossbit`, `latch cond`). At **18:45** a nine-seat
> > panel found a DIFFERENT defect (the row set was site-asymmetric), the addition list was
> > rewritten to carry the panel's findings, and **both earlier rows silently vanished.** The
> > measuring seat then BUILT both mutants and both escaped the committed 13-row detector --

 succeeded in 2646ms:
        "exit(1)s. PRE is `dead' -- EOF ALONE -- and that FAIL is the reproduction. #562: "
        "if=0 pins FIELD-ONLY (the write clears I/F; a whole-op decline keeps if=3)."
        % int(key[2:], 16))
    t = early + late
    diagnosis.append("%s: %s" % (nm, "load mode %d seen" % dec
                                 if ("arm_load_register_bank: unimplemented mode %d" % dec) in t
                                 else "NO LOAD TEXT"))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S5: save 2s
#  R3: the spec's slots -- post-MSR1 CPSR at DEST+4, return CPSR at DEST+8, SP at DEST+12.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_14", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "str_sp_c"],
                      [1, 2, 1, 3])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("save 2s", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 12, rt_at=DEST + 8),
    "m=13 sp once rt",
    "#0x14 then #0x13 in one process. On HEAD it dies on the FIRST MSR (the same Load death as "
    "kill 14). A Load-only fix still dies on the SECOND (Save of the reserved mode). Keep kill 15.")
t = early + late
diagnosis.append("save 2s: load=%s save=%s" % (
    "yes" if "arm_load_register_bank: unimplemented mode 20" in t else "no",
    "yes" if "arm_save_register_bank: unimplemented mode 20" in t else "no"))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S6..S13: accept side
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_10", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("acc usr", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8), "m=10 sp0",
    "USR32 is ONE-WAY (the USR mask strips the c-byte). SP is the USR bank's (0), so ` sp' in "
    "the got means the bank Load did not happen.")

for nm, key, want_m in (("acc fiq", "c_11", 0x11), ("acc irq", "c_12", 0x12),
                        ("acc abt", "c_17", 0x17), ("acc und", "c_1b", 0x1B),
                        ("acc sys", "c_1f", 0x1F)):
    cmds, full = msr_cmds([key, "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8",
                           "c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "str_sp_10"], [8],
                          late_end=DEST + 16)
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    if not marked or not arming(early, full):
        got = "unarmed"
    elif not alive:
        got = "dead"
    else:
        arrive, sp_arr = dumped(late, DEST + 4), dumped(late, DEST + 8)
        back, sp_back = dumped(late, DEST + 12), dumped(late, DEST + 16)
        got = "%s/%s/%s/%s" % (
            "m=%02x" % (arrive & 0x1F) if arrive not in (None, POISON) else "nofire",
            "0" if sp_arr == 0 else "x",
            "m=%02x" % (back & 0x1F) if back not in (None, POISON) else "nofire",
            "sp" if sp_back == BOOT_SP else "nosp")
    row(nm, got, "m=%02x/0/m=13/sp" % want_m,
        "ACCEPT + CANARY in one row: arrive mode/SP==0 (banked, BSS-zero), return mode 0x13 with "
        "SP==BOOT_SP. A Save no-op returns SP=0; a Load no-op arrives with SP=BOOT_SP.")

cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("same svc", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8), "m=13 sp",
    "SAME-MODE: Load is not called, so SP-UNCHANGED *IS* the preservation check. Not 0xd3 -- "
    "#0x13 clears I|F.")

cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_10", "c_14", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4"], [4])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("usr mask", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full), "m=10",
    "In USR32 the c-byte is STRIPPED, so the following #0x14 must NOT reach the mode field. "
    "The host lives and the mode stays 0x10.")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 rows
#  kill 1e: pins the helper's DOMAIN past the two driven reserved values -- a range form
#  admitting 0x1c-0x1e sends 0x1e to Load's backstop and reads m=1e.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_1e", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [1, 3])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("kill 1e", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key="msr_imm",
                        pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
    "MSR CPSR_c, #0x1e: unmodelled, like 0x14/0x15. Kills a helper widened by RANGE to 0x1e.")
diag_unimpl("kill 1e", early + late)

#  kill 03: the only driven mode value with bit 4 CLEAR.  Every other MSR value here has bit 4
#  set, so a decline that cleared only M[3:0] from the mask (`& ~0x0f') still wrote bit 4 = 0 from
#  this value and reached Load's backstop with mode 0x03; the rows before this one could not see it.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_03", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [1, 3])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("kill 03", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key="msr_imm",
                        pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
    "MSR CPSR_c, #0x03 (bit 4 clear): declined, mode unchanged. Pins the full 5-bit mode mask.")
diag_unimpl("kill 03", early + late)

#  The two SPSR restores.  `MSR SPSR_c, #0x14' poisons spsr_svc (I=F=0, mode 0x14); the restore
#  must land at TGT (else `nofire') in SVC32 -- current mode kept -- with I/F FROM THE SPSR
#  (if=0; a restore that skipped the CPSR write keeps the boot if=3) and the SVC SP.
for nm, op, site in (("rst movs", "movs_pc_lr", "dpi S-to-pc"),
                     ("rst ldm", "ldm_r5_pc_s", "arm_pop")):
    cmds, full = msr_cmds(["s_14", op], [6], restore=True)
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    row(nm, classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, once_key=site,
                     pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
        "MSR SPSR_c,#0x14 then %s: the restore reaches Load's default: (HEAD dead). Post-fix: "
        "at TGT, mode kept, I/F from the SPSR, `%s' announce." % (op, site))
    diag_unimpl(nm, early + late)

#  zero spsr: NO MSR at all -- spsr_svc is never written on testarm (cpu_new zero-fills), so
#  `MOVS pc,lr' from boot SVC32 restores mode 0x00.
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["movs_pc_lr"], [4], restore=True)
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("zero spsr", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8,
                          once_key="dpi S-to-pc", pin_if=True), "m=13 if=0 sp once",
    "MOVS pc,lr from boot SVC32 with spsr_svc never written (mode 0x00). HEAD dead.")
diag_unimpl("zero spsr", early + late)

#  The restores' ACCEPT side: a modelled SPSR mode must be APPLIED.  A guard made unconditional
#  (always keep the current mode) reads m=13 here.  SYS32 SP is the usr/sys bank's (0).
for nm, op in (("acc movs", "movs_pc_lr"), ("acc ldm", "ldm_r5_pc_s")):
    cmds, full = msr_cmds(["s_1f", op], [6], restore=True)
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    row(nm, classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, pin_if=True),
        "m=1f if=0 sp0",
        "MSR SPSR_c,#0x1f then %s: SYS32 restored, SP from the usr/sys bank (0), no announce."
        % op)

#  The BACKSTOPS: reached only with an unmodelled mode already in CPSR, which only the debugger
#  `cpsr=' ASSIGNMENT can now install (no bank switch, no flags
#  sync).  NOT `put', which writes MEMORY.  The poke follows the arming dumps.  once_key names
#  a DECLINE site that must NOT announce: a plant is not a guest decline (dbg both: the DPI
#  guard runs only when an SPSR was copied, and mode 0x14 has none).
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_13", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"], [4], poke=["cpsr=0xd4"])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("dbg save", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, backstops=True,
                         once_key="msr_imm"),
    "m=13 sp0 save",
    "cpsr=0xd4 then MSR CPSR_c,#0x13: Save's default: (HEAD exit(1)) announces and returns; "
    "Load(SVC) gives the never-saved svc SP: an actual 0, ` sp0'.")
diag_unimpl("dbg save", early + late)

cmds, full = msr_cmds(["movs_pc_lr"], [4], restore=True, poke=["cpsr=0xd4"])
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
row("dbg both", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, backstops=True,
                         once_key="dpi S-to-pc"),
    "m=14 sp save load",
    "cpsr=0xd4 then MOVS pc,lr: no SPSR for 0x14, so BOTH Save's and Load's default: run in ONE "
    "instruction (Load's backstop and the distinct bits are ALSO carried by latch rpt).")
diag_unimpl("dbg both", early + late)

#  latch ind: ALL THREE guest declines in ONE process -- MSR CPSR_c,#0x14, then MSR SPSR_c,#0x14
#  and LDM {pc}^ (arm_pop) to TGT, whose MOVS pc,lr (DPI) restores the same poisoned spsr_svc to
#  TGT2.  Each site has its own mode_warned bit, so all three texts must appear; any two sharing a
#  bit print only the first.  (The other rows are each a fresh process and cannot see sharing.)
cmds, full = msr_cmds(["c_14", "s_14", "ldm_r5_pc_s"], [8], restore=True, chain=True)
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
#  once_key="register_bank": the generic ` once' token would be set by ANY of the three texts and
#  so carries nothing here; keyed on the backstops it pins their ABSENCE instead (no guest
#  decline may fall through to Save/Load).  The per-site ` msr pop dpi' tags are the channel.
row("latch ind", classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, sp_at=DEST + 8, pin_if=True,
                          once_key="register_bank", declines=True), "m=13 if=0 sp msr pop dpi",
    "MSR, LDM^ and MOVS declines in ONE session: three distinct latch bits, three texts.")
diag_unimpl("latch ind", early + late)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 pass 2: latch rpt
#  EVERY one of the five latch sites visited TWICE in ONE process.  Wants EXACTLY ONE announce per
#  site (a latch that never suppresses prints two; two sites sharing a bit print one between
#  them) AND the repair still applied on the second visit: the declines leave SVC32 (a=13/..),
#  the backstops leave the planted 0x14 alive (b=14/..).
#    CODE  c_14 c_15 (msr x2)  s_14  ldm r5 -> T1      T1  ldm r6 -> T2  (pop x2)
#    T2    movs pc,lr -> T3                            T3  add lr,#0x40 ; movs -> T4  (dpi x2)
#    T4    mrs ; str [r4,#4] ; str sp,[r4,#8]
#  then cpsr=0xd4, pc=T5, lr=T6:
#    T5    movs pc,lr -> T6  (save+load)   T6  add lr,#0x40 ; movs -> T7  (save+load again)
#    T7    mrs ; str [r4,#12] ; str sp,[r4,#16]
LR1, LR2, LR3, LR4, LR5, LR6, LR7 = 0x8100, 0x8140, 0x8180, 0x81C0, 0x8200, 0x8240, 0x8280
early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
    ["c_14", "c_15", "s_14", "ldm_r5_pc_s"],
    blocks=[(LR1, ["ldm_r6_pc_s"]), (LR2, ["movs_pc_lr"]), (LR3, ["add_lr_40", "movs_pc_lr"]),
            (LR4, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"]), (LR5, ["movs_pc_lr"]),
            (LR6, ["add_lr_40", "movs_pc_lr"]), (LR7, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "str_sp_10"])],
    data={LDMW: LR1, LDMW + 4: LR2},
    regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "r6=0x%x" % (LDMW + 4), "lr=0x%x" % LR3],
    segments=(((), 11), (["cpsr=0xd4", "pc=0x%x" % LR5, "lr=0x%x" % LR6], 6)),
    late_end=DEST + 16)
if not marked or not arming(early, full):
    got = "unarmed"
elif not alive:
    got = "dead"
else:
    def _half(ca, sa):
        c, v = dumped(late, ca), dumped(late, sa)
        if c in (None, POISON):
            return "nofire"
        return "%02x/%d/%s" % (c & 0x1F, (c >> 6) & 3,
                               "sp" if v == BOOT_SP else ("0" if v == 0 else "x"))
    t = early + late
    got = "a=%s b=%s msr%d pop%d dpi%d save%d load%d" % (
        _half(DEST + 4, DEST + 8), _half(DEST + 12, DEST + 16),
        announces(t, "msr_imm"), announces(t, "arm_pop"), announces(t, "dpi S-to-pc"),
        announces(t, "arm_save_register_bank"), announces(t, "arm_load_register_bank"))
row("latch rpt", got, "a=13/0/sp b=14/3/sp msr1 pop1 dpi1 save1 load1",
    "Five latch sites x2 in ONE process: exactly one announce each, repair kept on visit 2.")
diag_unimpl("latch rpt", early + late)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 3: src dpi / src pop
#  CLASS CLOSURE of label x latch x outcome x masked bits (see the header).  Per label and path,
#  ONE process, four chained restores, each read back:
#    enter the label with I|F set; SPSR := N|Z|0xdf; restore     -> ACCEPT, latch clear  (R1)
#    re-enter it with I|F CLEAR;  SPSR_c := 0xcf;   restore     -> DECLINE, latch clear (R2)
#                                                    restore     -> DECLINE, latch SET   (R3)
#                                 SPSR_c := 0xdf;   restore     -> ACCEPT, latch set    (R4)
#  DPI chains through `movs pc,r5' (lr is banked; r5 is not) with r5 += 0x40 per block; POP chains
#  through `ldmia r6!,{pc}^' over a table of block addresses.  Want R1 = R4 = ffffffdf and R2 = R3
#  = ffffffc0|label (mode kept, every other bit from the SPSR 0xffffffcf; `msr cpsr_fsx,#0' and the
#  label re-entry before each decline leave the old CPSR 0x0000000m), and EXACTLY ONE announce.
BLK = [TGT + 0x40 * i for i in range(4)]     # 0x8100 0x8140 0x8180 0x81c0
for lab, mode in (("fiq", 0x11), ("irq", 0x12), ("abt", 0x17), ("und", 0x1B), ("svc", 0x13)):
    ck = "c_%02x" % (0xC0 | mode)            # first entry: I|F SET (helper operand bits 7:6)
    cc = "c_%02x" % mode                     # re-entry: I|F CLEAR, so the declines' I/F (set in
                                             # the SPSR) can only have come from the SPSR
    for path in ("dpi", "pop"):
        if path == "dpi":
            early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
                [ck, "sf_ff", "ss_ff", "sx_ff", "s_df", "movs_pc_r5"],
                blocks=[(BLK[0], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "add_r5_40", "cf_0", cc, "s_cf",
                                  "movs_pc_r5"]),
                        (BLK[1], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "add_r5_40", "cf_0", cc, "movs_pc_r5"]),
                        (BLK[2], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "add_r5_40", "s_df", "movs_pc_r5"]),
                        (BLK[3], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
                regs=["r5=0x%x" % BLK[0]],
                segments=(((), 26),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
            key = "dpi S-to-pc"
        else:
            early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
                [ck, "sf_ff", "ss_ff", "sx_ff", "s_df", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"],
                blocks=[(BLK[0], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "cf_0", cc, "s_cf", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
                        (BLK[1], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_8", "cf_0", cc, "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
                        (BLK[2], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "s_df", "ldm_r6w_pc_s"]),
                        (BLK[3], ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
                data={LDMW + 4 * i: BLK[i] for i in range(4)},
                regs=["r6=0x%x" % LDMW],
                segments=(((), 23),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
            key = "arm_pop"
        if not marked or not arming(early, full):
            got = "unarmed"
        elif not alive:
            got = "dead"
        else:
            vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8, 12, 16)]
            got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%08x" % v for v in vals) \
                + " n=%d" % announces(early + late, key)
        acc, dec = 0xFFFFFFDF, 0xFFFFFFC0 | mode
        row("src %s %s" % (path, lab), got, "%08x %08x %08x %08x n=1" % (acc, dec, dec, acc),
            "%s from %s: accept, decline, decline (latch set), accept; one announce."
            % (path.upper(), lab.upper()))
        diag_unimpl("src %s %s" % (path, lab), early + late)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 3: msr lbl
#  The msr_imm guard at every current-mode label it can run in (USR strips the c-byte): enter
#  FIQ, IRQ, ABT, UND, SYS in turn WITH I|F set, and in each write #0x14.  Each decline keeps the
#  label and clears I/F (the c-byte 0x14 has them clear).  One announce in total.
seq, blocks = [], []
for i, m in enumerate((0x11, 0x12, 0x17, 0x1B, 0x1F)):
    seq += ["c_%02x" % (0xC0 | m), "c_14", "mrs_r3", ("str_r3_4", "str_r3_8", "str_r3_c",
                                                      "str_r3_10", "str_r3_14")[i]]
early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(seq, segments=(((), 20),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
if not marked or not arming(early, full):
    got = "unarmed"
elif not alive:
    got = "dead"
else:
    vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8, 12, 16, 20)]
    got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%02x" % v for v in vals) \
        + " n=%d" % announces(early + late, "msr_imm")
row("msr lbl", got, "11 12 17 1b 1f n=1",
    "MSR CPSR_c,#0x14 from FIQ IRQ ABT UND SYS: declined at every label, one announce.")
diag_unimpl("msr lbl", early + late)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 3: encl
#  The restores' ENCLOSING predicates.  SVC with spsr_svc = 0x1f (a VALID, different mode), then
#  three PC writes that must NOT restore it: a plain `ldmia r5,{pc}' (return_flag 0), a non-S
#  `add pc,r6,#0' (the A__PC template instance, not the `ret' shortcut that `mov pc,lr' decodes
#  to), and `add r0,pc,#0' (Rn = PC, Rd != PC: no PC write at all).  Wants m=13 after each and
#  r0 = its own address + 8.
E1, E2 = TGT, TGT + 0x40
early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
    ["s_1f", "ldm_r5_pc"],
    blocks=[(E1, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "add_pc_r6_0"]),
            (E2, ["add_r0_pc_0", "str_r0_c", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_8"])],
    data={LDMW: E1}, regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "r6=0x%x" % E2],
    segments=(((), 9),), late_end=DEST + 0x10)
if not marked or not arming(early, full):
    got = "unarmed"
elif not alive:
    got = "dead"
else:
    m1, m2, r0 = dumped(late, DEST + 4), dumped(late, DEST + 8), dumped(late, DEST + 12)
    got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else f(v)
                   for v, f in ((m1, lambda x: "%02x" % (x & 0x1F)),
                                (m2, lambda x: "%02x" % (x & 0x1F)), (r0, lambda x: "%x" % x)))
row("encl", got, "13 13 %x" % (E2 + 8),
    "Plain LDM {pc}, non-S ADD pc, ADD r0,pc with spsr_svc = 0x1f: none may restore the SPSR.")

T2, T3, T4, T5 = 0x8200, 0x8300, 0x8340, 0x8380
T6 = 0x83C0
early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
    ["s_3f", "ldm_r5_pc_s"],
    blocks=[(TGT, []), (T2, ["s_3f", "movs_pc_lr"]), (T3, ["c_3f"]), (T4, ["c_34"]),
            (T5, ["s_34", "ldm_r5_pc_s"]), (T6, ["s_34", "movs_pc_lr"])],
    data={LDMW: TGT}, regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "lr=0x%x" % TGT],
    segments=(((), 2), (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T2, "lr=0x%x" % TGT], 2),
              (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T3], 1),
              (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T4], 1),
              (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T5], 2),
              (["print cpsr", "cpsr=0xd3", "pc=0x%x" % T6, "lr=0x%x" % TGT], 2),
              (["print cpsr"], 0)))
if not marked or not arming(early, full):
    got = "unarmed"
elif not alive:
    got = "dead"
else:
    vals = re.findall(r"print\s+cpsr\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)", late)
    t = early + late
    got = " ".join("%02x" % (int(v, 16) & 0xff) for v in vals) + " n=%d" % (
        announces(t, "msr_imm") + announces(t, "arm_pop") + announces(t, "dpi S-to-pc"))
row("tbit", got, "3f 3f 3f 33 33 33 n=3",
    "T-set accept via pop/DPI/msr (0x3f), T-set decline via msr/pop/DPI (0x34): T from the operand.")
T7 = 0x8400
early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
    ["s_1f", "adds_r0_pc_0", "mov_r0_2a", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "ldm_r5_r0_s", "mrs_r3",
     "str_r3_8", "s_14", "subs_pc_lr_0"],
    blocks=[(T7, ["mrs_r3", "str_r3_c", "c_11", "c_03", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_10"])],
    data={LDMW: 0x12345678}, regs=["r5=0x%x" % LDMW, "lr=0x%x" % T7],
    segments=(((), 16),), late_end=DEST + 0x14)
if not marked or not arming(early, full):
    got = "unarmed"
elif not alive:
    got = "dead"
else:
    vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8, 12, 16)]
    got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%02x" % (v & 0x1f) for v in vals)
row("encl2", got, "13 13 13 11",
    "adds r0,pc / ldm {r0}^ must not restore; subs pc,lr declines 0x14; msr #0x03 at FIQ declined.")
#  msr wide: the msr_imm path's operand bits 31:8 (only the register form reaches them).  ACCEPT
#  r0 = 0xffffffd1 (FIQ, every bit but T and mode bits 1-3): a widened helper projection declines
#  it.  Then, from FIQ, DECLINE r1 = 0x00000014: field-only, so every non-mode bit comes from the
#  operand -- bits 31:6 differ from the old CPSR -- and the mode stays FIQ: want 0x00000011.
early, late, alive, marked, full = prog_session(
    ["msr_fsxc_r0", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "msr_fsxc_r1", "mrs_r3", "str_r3_8"],
    regs=["r0=0xffffffd1", "r1=0x14"], segments=(((), 6),), late_end=DEST + 0x10)
if not marked or not arming(early, full):
    got = "unarmed"
elif not alive:
    got = "dead"
else:
    vals = [dumped(late, DEST + o) for o in (4, 8)]
    got = " ".join("nofire" if v in (None, POISON) else "%08x" % v for v in vals) \
        + " n=%d" % announces(early + late, "msr_imm")
row("msr wide", got, "ffffffd1 00000011 n=1",
    "MSR CPSR_fsxc,r0: accept 0xffffffd1 (all operand bits 31:6), decline 0x14 field-only.")
diag_unimpl("msr wide", early + late)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- #562 repair 5: the GENERATED GRID
#  Owner ruling 2026-10-08: the restore/MSR detector rows are GENERATED from the full space, not
#  written by hand.  Three sessions (one per path: msr_imm, DPI S-to-PC, arm_pop), each a list of
#  OBSERVATIONS produced by grid_obs() below.  One observation:
#      debugger:  cpsr=0xd3 (re-arm; a previous result may have T set), pc=<block>, r0=OLD, r1=NEW
#      guest:     msr cpsr_fsxc,r0           -- OLD CPSR, a modelled label, set by a real MSR
#                                               (bank switch and `flags' both correct)
#                 msr spsr_fsxc,r1           -- (restore paths only) SPSR := NEW
#                 the instruction under test -- msr cpsr_fsxc,r1 / movs pc,r2 / ldmia r6,{pc}^
#                                               (pop also: ldmia r6,{pc}, ldmia r6,{r0}^, ldmia r6,{r0})
#      debugger:  print cpsr                 -- the WHOLE result word, compared exactly with WANT
#  WANT is computed: accept -> NEW; decline -> (NEW & ~0x1f) | label; a non-restoring pop form
#  -> OLD.  The first decline in a session runs with the site's latch CLEAR, every later one with
#  it SET; the site must announce exactly once and no Save/Load backstop text may appear.
#
#  G1 per path x outcome x bit 0..31 x polarity: observations come in COMPLEMENTARY PAIRS --
#     p1: OLD = label (bits 31:5 all 0)          NEW = 0xffffffe0 | mode (bits 31:5 all 1, T SET)
#     p2: OLD = 0xffffffc0 | label (31:6 all 1)  NEW = mode (bits 31:5 all 0)
#     so for every non-mode bit both result polarities are observed with the OLD value different
#     (keep-old, force-0 and force-1 all change the word).  T cannot be 1 in OLD (the guest runs
#     ARM code), so T polarity 0 is observed only with OLD T = 0 -- keep-old T is caught at p1.
#  G2 MSR label x mode bit: each label also declines label ^ 0x1f, which differs in EVERY mode bit
#     (and is always unmodelled, since bit 4 is cleared); on the restore paths the same value is
#     the declined SPSR, so a keep-mode mask that lets any SPSR mode bit through changes the word.
#  G3 helper boundary: ALL 25 unmodelled values (0x00-0x0f, 0x14-0x16, 0x18-0x1a, 0x1c-0x1e) are
#     declined on EVERY path, labels cycling; ALL 7 modelled modes are accepted on every path.
#     A narrowed projection (e.g. `& 0x17' admits 0x18-0x1a) or widened one changes some word.
#  G4 pop enclosing cells: {S, non-S} x {PC, no PC} all four, both polarities.
#  The `grid cov' row re-derives the bit x polarity table from the generated observations and
#  reds if any cell outside the named equivalents is uncovered.
MODELLED = (0x10, 0x11, 0x12, 0x13, 0x17, 0x1B, 0x1F)
UNMODELLED = tuple(m for m in range(32) if m not in MODELLED)          # 25 values
RESTORE_LABELS = (0x11, 0x12, 0x13, 0x17, 0x1B)                        # SPSR-bearing
MSR_LABELS = (0x11, 0x12, 0x13, 0x17, 0x1B, 0x1F)                      # USR strips the c-byte
WORDS["msr_spsr_fsxc_r1"] = 0xE16FF001       # msr spsr_fsxc, r1
WORDS["movs_pc_r2"] = 0xE1B0F002             # movs pc, r2
WORDS["ldm_r6_pc"] = 0xE8968000              # ldmia r6, {pc}        (non-S, PC)
WORDS["ldm_r6_r0_s"] = 0xE8D60001            # ldmia r6, {r0}^       (S, no PC)
WORDS["ldm_r6_r0"] = 0xE8960001              # ldmia r6, {r0}        (non-S, no PC)
GRID_CHECK = {"msr_spsr_fsxc_r1": lambda w: w & 0x0FFFFFF0 == 0x016FF000 and w & 0xF == 1,
              "movs_pc_r2": lambda w: (w >> 21) & 0xF == 0xD and (w >> 20) & 1
              and (w >> 12) & 0xF == 15 and w & 0xFFF == 2 and (w >> 25) & 7 == 0,
              "ldm_r6_pc": lambda w: w & 0x0FFFFFFF == 0x08968000,
              "ldm_r6_r0_s": lambda w: w & 0x0FFFFFFF == 0x08D60001,
              "ldm_r6_r0": lambda w: w & 0x0FFFFFFF == 0x08960001}
GBAD = [k for k, f in GRID_CHECK.items() if not f(WORDS[k]) or WORDS[k] >> 28 != 0xE]
for k in ("ldm_r6_pc_s",):
    if WORDS[k] & 0x0FFFFFFF != 0x08D68000:
        GBAD.append(k)

HI1, HI0 = 0xFFFFFFE0, 0xFFFFFFC0             # bits 31:5 set (T set) / bits 31:6 set (T clear)


def grid_obs(path):
    """[(kind, label, OLD, NEW, WANT)] for one path -- the whole generated table."""
    labels = MSR_LABELS if path == "msr" else RESTORE_LABELS
    obs = []

    def add(kind, lab, new, pol):
        old = lab if pol == 1 else (HI0 | lab)
        newv = (HI1 | new) if pol == 1 else new
        if kind == "acc":
            want = newv
        elif kind == "dec":
            want = (newv & ~0x1F) | lab
        else:                                    # non-restoring pop forms
            want = old
        obs.append((kind, lab, old, newv, want))

    i = 0
    for lab in labels:                           # G2 complement declines, both polarities
        for pol in (1, 2):
            add("dec", lab, lab ^ 0x1F, pol)
    for m in UNMODELLED:                         # G3 every unmodelled value
        add("dec", labels[i % len(labels)], m, 1 + (i % 2))
        i += 1
    j = 0
    for lab in labels:                           # accepts: every modelled mode, both polarities,

 succeeded in 2691ms:
        os._exit(127)
    buf = ""
    eof = False

    def rd(t):
        nonlocal buf, eof
        end = time.time() + t
        while time.time() < end:
            try:
                r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.1)
            except OSError:
                eof = True
                return
            if not r:
                continue
            try:
                d = os.read(fd, 65536)
            except OSError:
                eof = True
                return
            if not d:
                eof = True
                return
            buf += d.decode("utf-8", "replace")

    rd(settle)
    for c in cmds:
        if eof:
            break
        try:
            os.write(fd, (c + "\n").encode())
        except OSError:
            eof = True
            break
        rd(0.3)
    rd(spin)

    #  *** POLL, NEVER SAMPLE ONCE. ***  rd() returns immediately on EOF, so a dying child must
    #  be given time to die.  On EOF reap with a long deadline; without EOF a short one (R7).
    alive = True
    deadline = time.time() + (12.0 if eof else 3.0)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        try:
            wpid, _st = os.waitpid(pid, os.WNOHANG)
        except ChildProcessError:
            alive = False
            break
        if wpid == pid:
            alive = False
            break
        time.sleep(0.05)
    if alive:
        try:
            os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        except OSError:
            pass
        try:
            _w, st = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
            #  R7: if the child exited on its own between the last poll and our kill, the
            #  status says so -- do not leave alive=True for a process that died by itself.
            if os.WIFEXITED(st) or (os.WIFSIGNALED(st) and os.WTERMSIG(st) != signal.SIGKILL):
                alive = False
        except ChildProcessError:
            pass
    try:
        os.close(fd)
    except OSError:
        pass
    return buf.replace("\r", ""), alive


def _session_split(cmds):
    #  Insert a unique, harmless marker command at MARK so the split survives \r stripping:
    #  `print 0x5eed5eed' is answered by the debugger (sh_intprio_probe.py measured `print
    #  0x1234'), and even if it were not, its echo carries the token.
    real = []
    for c in cmds:
        real.append("print 0x5eed5eed" if c == MARK else c)
    t, alive = session(real)
    i = t.find("5eed5eed")
    if i < 0:
        return t, "", alive, False
    #  late = everything after the marker's answer line (skip echo + answer)
    j = t.rfind("5eed5eed")
    nl = t.find("\n", j)
    late = t[nl + 1:] if nl >= 0 else ""
    return t[:i], late, alive, True


def printed(t, reg):
    #  COPIED VERBATIM from regress/lca_isalane_probe.py.  `print <reg>' emits a BARE `0x...'.
    m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
    return None if not m else int(m.group(1), 16)


def dumpcmd(addr, end=None):
    """*** ALWAYS DUMP FROM THE 16-BYTE-ALIGNED BASE. ***  debugger_cmd_dump does
    `addr = addr_start & ~0xf' and blank-pads groups before addr_start (arm_bdt_probe.py
    read_mem32(), measured 2026-09-11)."""
    base = addr & ~0xF
    top = ((end if end is not None else addr) + 16) & ~0xF
    if top <= base:
        top = base + 16
    return "dump 0x%x 0x%x" % (base, top)


def dumped(t, addr):
    """The word stored at `addr', from the LAST aligned dump line in `t' (R2: callers pass the
    early or the late half, never the whole transcript).  Memory-order bytes, little-endian
    guest: assemble like sw() in arm_flags_probe.py."""
    label = "0x%08x" % (addr & ~0xF)
    idx = (addr & 0xF) // 4
    val = None
    for line in t.splitlines():
        if line.startswith(label):
            parts = line[12:48].split()
            if len(parts) == 4 and len(parts[idx]) == 8:
                val = int.from_bytes(bytes.fromhex(parts[idx]), "little")
    return val


def plant(ops, at=CODE):
    out = []
    for i, k in enumerate(ops):
        out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, WORDS[k]))
    out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), WORDS["b_self"]))
    return out


def prologue():
    """POISON DEST..DEST+16 (the post-MSR stores must overwrite them) and point r4 at DEST."""
    return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (DEST + o, POISON) for o in (0, 4, 8, 12, 16)] \
        + ["r4=0x%x" % DEST]


PREFIX = ["mrs_r3", "str_r3"]


def msr_cmds(ops, steps_after, late_end=DEST + 12, restore=False, regs=(), poke=(),
             chain=False):
    """Standard MSR session: prefix MRS/STR to DEST, arming dumps (DEST and the whole planted
    stream), MARK, the stimulus steps, then the result dump.
    #562: restore=True also plants TGT_OPS at TGT, sets lr=TGT and the LDMW word to TGT, and
    arms all three (a fall-through past the restore lands on b_self and reads `nofire').
    `regs' are debugger assignments made BEFORE the prefix; `poke' ones AFTER the arming dumps
    (a `cpsr=' poke must follow the prefix, whose MRS arms DEST == 0xd3).
    chain=True (with restore): TGT holds `movs pc,lr' and the readback TGT_OPS move to TGT2,
    where lr points -- so one session can run an LDM^ restore and then a MOVS restore."""
    full = PREFIX + ops
    cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
    extra = {}
    rb = TGT2 if chain else TGT
    if restore:
        cmds += plant(TGT_OPS, at=rb) + ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (LDMW, TGT),
                                         "lr=0x%x" % rb, "r5=0x%x" % LDMW]
        for i, k in enumerate(TGT_OPS + ["b_self"]):
            extra[rb + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
        extra[LDMW] = TGT
        if chain:
            cmds += plant(["movs_pc_lr"], at=TGT)
            extra[TGT] = WORDS["movs_pc_lr"]
            extra[TGT + 4] = WORDS["b_self"]
    cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
                          dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
    if restore:
        cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT, TGT + 4 * len(TGT_OPS)), dumpcmd(LDMW)]
        if chain:
            cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT2, TGT2 + 4 * len(TGT_OPS))]
    cmds += [MARK] + list(poke) + ["step %d" % s for s in steps_after] \
        + [dumpcmd(DEST, late_end)]
    return cmds, (full, extra)


def arming(early, full):
    """R4: DEST == 0xd3 after the prefix, and every planted word reads back as its WORDS entry.
    #562: `full' may be (ops, {addr: word}) -- the extra plants (restore target, LDM word)."""
    extra = {}
    if isinstance(full, tuple):
        full, extra = full
    if dumped(early, DEST) != BOOT_CPSR:
        return False
    for i, k in enumerate(full):
        if dumped(early, CODE + 4 * i) != WORDS[k]:
            return False
    for a, w in extra.items():
        if dumped(early, a) != w:
            return False
    return True


def prog_session(code_ops, blocks=(), data=None, regs=(), segments=((), ()), late_end=DEST + 12):
    """#562 pass 2: a multi-block program.  code_ops at CODE (after PREFIX), each (addr, ops) block
    at addr, each {addr: word} data word planted; every block and data word is ARMED (dumped and
    checked before MARK).  `segments' = ((pokes, steps), ...) after MARK: debugger assignments,
    then `step N'."""
    data = data or {}
    full = PREFIX + list(code_ops)
    cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
    extra = {}
    for a, ops in blocks:
        cmds += plant(ops, at=a)
        for i, k in enumerate(list(ops) + ["b_self"]):
            extra[a + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
    for a, w in data.items():
        cmds.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (a, w))
        extra[a] = w
    cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
                          dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
    for a, ops in blocks:
        cmds.append(dumpcmd(a, a + 4 * len(ops)))
    for a in data:
        cmds.append(dumpcmd(a))
    cmds.append(MARK)
    for pokes, steps in segments:
        cmds += list(pokes) + (["step %d" % steps] if steps else [])
    cmds.append(dumpcmd(DEST, late_end))
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    return early, late, alive, marked, (full, extra)


def announces(t, key):
    """#562 pass 2: how MANY `(once)' lines the named site printed -- a latch that never
    suppresses prints twice when its site is visited twice; any() could not see that."""
    return sum(1 for ln in t.splitlines() if key in ln and "(once)" in ln)


def diag_unimpl(nm, t):
    """#562: which register-bank `unimplemented mode' text a session printed (HEAD: the
    function that exit(1)ed -- the reason under test; fixed: the backstop that announced)."""
    hits = sorted(set(re.findall(r"arm_(save|load)_register_bank: unimplemented mode (\d+)", t)))
    diagnosis.append("%s: %s" % (nm, ", ".join("%s mode %s" % h for h in hits) if hits
                                 else "no bank text"))


def announced(t, key):
    """#562: a `(once)' announce from the NAMED site -- the five sites share the token, so a
    bare `(once)' cannot tell a decline from a backstop, or Save's backstop from Load's."""
    return any(key in ln and "(once)" in ln for ln in t.splitlines())


def classify(early, late, alive, marked, full, cpsr_at=DEST + 4, sp_at=None, rt_at=None,
             once_key=None, pin_if=False, backstops=False, declines=False):
    """The got string.  EOF ALONE => dead.  Arming is checked first, from the EARLY half.
    #562: once_key names the announcing site; pin_if adds the CPSR I/F pair (field-only vs
    whole-operation decline); backstops adds ` save' / ` load' per Save/Load announce."""
    if not marked or not arming(early, full):
        return "unarmed"
    if not alive:
        return "dead"
    cpsr = dumped(late, cpsr_at)
    if cpsr is None or cpsr == POISON:
        return "nofire"
    got = "m=%02x" % (cpsr & 0x1F)
    if pin_if:
        got += " if=%d" % ((cpsr >> 6) & 3)
    if sp_at is not None:
        v = dumped(late, sp_at)
        #  #562 pass 2: ` sp0' is an ACTUAL zero readback (a never-loaded bank), not merely
        #  "not BOOT_SP" -- a missing or POISON slot now renders neither token.
        if v == BOOT_SP:
            got += " sp"
        elif v == 0:
            got += " sp0"
    t = early + late
    if (announced(t, once_key) if once_key else "(once)" in t):
        got += " once"
    if declines:
        for key, tag in (("msr_imm", "msr"), ("arm_pop", "pop"), ("dpi S-to-pc", "dpi")):
            if announced(t, key):
                got += " " + tag
    if backstops:
        if announced(t, "arm_save_register_bank"):
            got += " save"
        if announced(t, "arm_load_register_bank"):
            got += " load"
    if rt_at is not None:
        v = dumped(late, rt_at)
        if v not in (None, POISON) and (v & 0x1F) == 0x13:
            got += " rt"
    return got


def report_dead(why):
    print("arm_msrmode_probe -- binary=%s label=%s machine=testarm" % (BIN, LABEL))
    print("ARMMSRMODE_CONTROL=DEAD")
    print("ARMMSRMODE_RESULT=0/0")
    print("  *** SETUP FAILED (%s) -- the vehicle is not the vehicle.  NOT 'no defect'. ***" % why)
    for nm, got, want, note in rows:
        print("  %-11s got=%-16s want=%-16s %s" % (nm, got, want, note[:56]))
    print("ARMMSRMODE_DONE")
    raise SystemExit(0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- enc ok  (no guest)
#  MSR immediate:  cond 00110 R 10 field(19:16) 1111 rot(11:8) imm8.  The field nibble is
#  bits 19:16 and a mask cannot substitute for it (0x0FB0F000 kills bit 0x08 and cannot tell
#  the c-form from the f-form).
bad = []
for k, w in WORDS.items():
    if k.startswith("c_"):
        if ((w >> 16) & 0xF) != 0b0001 or (w & 0xFF) != int(k[2:], 16) \
                or ((w >> 8) & 0xF) != 0 or (w & 0x00400000) or (w & 0x0FB0F000) != 0x0320F000:
            bad.append(k)
    if k == "f_14":
        if ((w >> 16) & 0xF) != 0b1000 or (w & 0xFF) != 0x14 or ((w >> 8) & 0xF) != 0 \
                or (w & 0x00400000) or (w & 0x0FB0F000) != 0x0320F000:
            bad.append(k)
    if k.startswith("s_"):
        #  #562: the SPSR form -- same frame, field nibble 0001, bit 22 (R) SET.
        if ((w >> 16) & 0xF) != 0b0001 or (w & 0xFF) != int(k[2:], 16) \
                or ((w >> 8) & 0xF) != 0 or not (w & 0x00400000) \
                or (w & 0x0FB0F000) != 0x0320F000:
            bad.append(k)
    if k == "movs_pc_lr":
        #  data-processing MOV (opcode 1101), S set, Rd = 15, Rm = 14, no shift
        if (w >> 21) & 0xF != 0xD or not (w >> 20) & 1 or (w >> 12) & 0xF != 15 \
                or w & 0xFFF != 14 or (w >> 25) & 7 != 0:
            bad.append(k)
    if k in ("ldm_r6_pc_s", "mov_lr_8100", "add_lr_40", "sf_c0"):
        #  #562 pass 2: whole-word field decode against the intended operation.
        ok = {"ldm_r6_pc_s": (w >> 25) & 7 == 0b100 and (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 22) & 1
              and not (w >> 21) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 6 and w & 0xFFFF == 0x8000,
              "mov_lr_8100": (w >> 25) & 7 == 1 and (w >> 21) & 0xF == 0xD
              and not (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 12) & 0xF == 14
              and (((w & 0xFF) >> (2 * ((w >> 8) & 0xF))) | ((w & 0xFF)
                   << (32 - 2 * ((w >> 8) & 0xF)))) & 0xFFFFFFFF == TGT,
              "add_lr_40": (w >> 25) & 7 == 1 and (w >> 21) & 0xF == 0x4
              and not (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 14 and (w >> 12) & 0xF == 14
              and w & 0xFFF == 0x40,
              "sf_c0": (w & 0x0FB0F000) == 0x0320F000 and (w & 0x00400000)
              and ((w >> 16) & 0xF) == 0b1000 and ((w >> 8) & 0xF) == 1 and (w & 0xFF) == 3,
              }[k]
        if not ok:
            bad.append(k)
    if k in ("sf_ff", "ss_ff", "sx_ff", "cf_0", "msr_fsxc_r0", "msr_fsxc_r1", "adds_r0_pc_0",
             "ldm_r5_r0_s", "subs_pc_lr_0"):
        #  #562 repair 4: decode the closure words field by field (rotated imm value, R, field mask,
        #  opcode/S/Rn/Rd, LDM bits) against the operation each key names.
        rot = (w >> 8) & 0xF
        val = ((w & 0xFF) >> (2 * rot) | (w & 0xFF) << (32 - 2 * rot)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        msri = (w & 0x0FB0F000) == 0x0320F000
        ok = {"sf_ff": msri and (w >> 22) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 8 and val == 0xFF000000,
              "ss_ff": msri and (w >> 22) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 4 and val == 0x00FF0000,
              "sx_ff": msri and (w >> 22) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 2 and val == 0x0000FF00,
              "cf_0": msri and not (w >> 22) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 0xE and val == 0,
              "msr_fsxc_r0": w & 0x0FFFFFF0 == 0x012FF000 and w & 0xF == 0,
              "msr_fsxc_r1": w & 0x0FFFFFF0 == 0x012FF000 and w & 0xF == 1,
              "adds_r0_pc_0": (w >> 25) & 7 == 1 and (w >> 21) & 0xF == 4 and (w >> 20) & 1
              and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 15 and (w >> 12) & 0xF == 0 and val == 0,
              "subs_pc_lr_0": (w >> 25) & 7 == 1 and (w >> 21) & 0xF == 2 and (w >> 20) & 1
              and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 14 and (w >> 12) & 0xF == 15 and val == 0,
              "ldm_r5_r0_s": (w >> 25) & 7 == 4 and (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 22) & 1
              and not (w >> 21) & 1 and (w >> 23) & 1 and not (w >> 24) & 1
              and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 5 and w & 0xFFFF == 0x0001}[k]
        if not ok or w >> 28 != 0xE:
            bad.append(k)
    if k in ("movs_pc_r5", "add_r5_40", "ldm_r6w_pc_s", "ldm_r5_pc", "add_pc_r6_0", "add_r0_pc_0",
             "str_r0_c", "str_r3_10", "str_r3_14"):
        #  #562 repair 3: cond, class, opcode, S/W/L, Rn, Rd and operand, field by field.
        f = ((w >> 28), (w >> 25) & 7, (w >> 21) & 0xF, (w >> 20) & 1, (w >> 16) & 0xF,
             (w >> 12) & 0xF, w & 0xFFF)
        want = {"movs_pc_r5": (0xE, 0, 0xD, 1, 0, 15, 5),
                "add_r5_40": (0xE, 1, 0x4, 0, 5, 5, 0x40),
                "add_pc_r6_0": (0xE, 1, 0x4, 0, 6, 15, 0),
                "add_r0_pc_0": (0xE, 1, 0x4, 0, 15, 0, 0),
                "str_r0_c": (0xE, 2, 0xC, 0, 4, 0, 0xC),
                "str_r3_10": (0xE, 2, 0xC, 0, 4, 3, 0x10),
                "str_r3_14": (0xE, 2, 0xC, 0, 4, 3, 0x14)}.get(k)
        if k == "ldm_r6w_pc_s":
            ok = (w >> 25) & 7 == 4 and (w >> 20) & 1 and (w >> 22) & 1 and (w >> 21) & 1 \
                and (w >> 23) & 1 and not (w >> 24) & 1 and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 6 \
                and w & 0xFFFF == 0x8000
        elif k == "ldm_r5_pc":
            ok = (w >> 25) & 7 == 4 and (w >> 20) & 1 and not (w >> 22) & 1 \
                and not (w >> 21) & 1 and (w >> 23) & 1 and not (w >> 24) & 1 \
                and (w >> 16) & 0xF == 5 and w & 0xFFFF == 0x8000
        else:
            ok = f == want
        if not ok:
            bad.append(k)
    if k == "ldm_r5_pc_s":
        #  LDM: bits 27:25 = 100, L set, S (bit 22) set, W clear, Rn = 5, list = {pc} only
        if (w >> 25) & 7 != 0b100 or not (w >> 20) & 1 or not (w >> 22) & 1 \
                or (w >> 21) & 1 or (w >> 16) & 0xF != 5 or w & 0xFFFF != 0x8000:
            bad.append(k)
row("enc ok", "clean" if not bad else ",".join(bad), "clean",
    "CONTROL=DEAD. Decoder match on the PLANTED literals: MSR-imm frame (w & 0x0FB0F000) == "
    "0x0320F000, field nibble (w>>16)&0xF = 0001 c-form / 1000 f-form, rot 0, bit 22 clear, imm8 "
    "== the key's mode.")
if bad:
    report_dead("enc ok")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1: live / pc adv
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(
    prologue() + plant(["mov_r0_2a", "str_r0"])
    + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST), "print pc"])
row("live", "0x%x" % (dumped(early, DEST) or 0) if alive else "dead", "0x2a",
    "CONTROL=DEAD. Plant, step and dump executed at all.")
row("pc adv", "0x%x" % (printed(early, "pc") or 0) if alive else "dead", "0x%x" % (CODE + 8),
    "CONTROL=DEAD. The words were translated as 4-byte ARM instructions.")
if rows[-2][1] != rows[-2][2] or rows[-1][1] != rows[-1][2]:
    report_dead("live/pc adv")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- S1b: armed
early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(
    prologue() + plant(["mrs_r3", "str_r3_4", "str_sp_8"])
    + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 3", dumpcmd(DEST, DEST + 8)])
a4, a8 = dumped(early, DEST + 4), dumped(early, DEST + 8)
armed = "%s/%s" % (("0x%x" % a4) if (alive and a4 is not None) else "?",
                   ("0x%x" % a8) if (alive and a8 is not None) else "?")
row("armed", armed, "0x%x/0x%x" % (BOOT_CPSR, BOOT_SP),
    "CONTROL=DEAD. SVC32 privileged testarm -- not USR32, not barearm, and not a debugger cpsr "
    "write. If this reds NO kill row may be scored.")

    def rd(t):
        nonlocal buf, eof
        end = time.time() + t
        while time.time() < end:
            try:
                r, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], 0.1)
            except OSError:
                eof = True
                return
            if not r:
                continue
            try:
                d = os.read(fd, 65536)
            except OSError:
                eof = True
                return
            if not d:
                eof = True
                return
            buf += d.decode("utf-8", "replace")

    for c in cmds:
        if eof:
            break
        try:
            os.write(fd, (c + "\n").encode())
        except OSError:
            eof = True
            break
        rd(0.3)
    rd(spin)

    #  *** POLL, NEVER SAMPLE ONCE. ***  rd() returns immediately on EOF, so a dying child must
    #  be given time to die.  On EOF reap with a long deadline; without EOF a short one (R7).
    alive = True
    deadline = time.time() + (12.0 if eof else 3.0)
    while time.time() < deadline:
        try:
            wpid, _st = os.waitpid(pid, os.WNOHANG)
        except ChildProcessError:
            alive = False
            break
        if wpid == pid:
            alive = False
            break
        time.sleep(0.05)
    if alive:
        try:
            os.kill(pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        except OSError:
            pass
        try:
            _w, st = os.waitpid(pid, 0)
            #  R7: if the child exited on its own between the last poll and our kill, the
            #  status says so -- do not leave alive=True for a process that died by itself.
            if os.WIFEXITED(st) or (os.WIFSIGNALED(st) and os.WTERMSIG(st) != signal.SIGKILL):
                alive = False
        except ChildProcessError:
            pass
    try:
        os.close(fd)
    except OSError:
        pass
    return buf.replace("\r", ""), alive


def _session_split(cmds):
    #  Insert a unique, harmless marker command at MARK so the split survives \r stripping:
    #  `print 0x5eed5eed' is answered by the debugger (sh_intprio_probe.py measured `print
    #  0x1234'), and even if it were not, its echo carries the token.
    real = []
    for c in cmds:
        real.append("print 0x5eed5eed" if c == MARK else c)
    t, alive = session(real)
    i = t.find("5eed5eed")
    if i < 0:
        return t, "", alive, False
    #  late = everything after the marker's answer line (skip echo + answer)
    j = t.rfind("5eed5eed")
    nl = t.find("\n", j)
    late = t[nl + 1:] if nl >= 0 else ""
    return t[:i], late, alive, True


def printed(t, reg):
    #  COPIED VERBATIM from regress/lca_isalane_probe.py.  `print <reg>' emits a BARE `0x...'.
    m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
    return None if not m else int(m.group(1), 16)


def dumpcmd(addr, end=None):
    """*** ALWAYS DUMP FROM THE 16-BYTE-ALIGNED BASE. ***  debugger_cmd_dump does
    `addr = addr_start & ~0xf' and blank-pads groups before addr_start (arm_bdt_probe.py
    read_mem32(), measured 2026-09-11)."""
    base = addr & ~0xF
    top = ((end if end is not None else addr) + 16) & ~0xF
    if top <= base:
        top = base + 16
    return "dump 0x%x 0x%x" % (base, top)


def dumped(t, addr):
    """The word stored at `addr', from the LAST aligned dump line in `t' (R2: callers pass the
    early or the late half, never the whole transcript).  Memory-order bytes, little-endian
    guest: assemble like sw() in arm_flags_probe.py."""
    label = "0x%08x" % (addr & ~0xF)
    idx = (addr & 0xF) // 4
    val = None
    for line in t.splitlines():
        if line.startswith(label):
            parts = line[12:48].split()
            if len(parts) == 4 and len(parts[idx]) == 8:
                val = int.from_bytes(bytes.fromhex(parts[idx]), "little")
    return val


def plant(ops, at=CODE):
    out = []
    for i, k in enumerate(ops):
        out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * i, WORDS[k]))
    out.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * len(ops), WORDS["b_self"]))
    return out


def prologue():
    """POISON DEST..DEST+16 (the post-MSR stores must overwrite them) and point r4 at DEST."""
    return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (DEST + o, POISON) for o in (0, 4, 8, 12, 16)] \
        + ["r4=0x%x" % DEST]


PREFIX = ["mrs_r3", "str_r3"]


def msr_cmds(ops, steps_after, late_end=DEST + 12, restore=False, regs=(), poke=(),
             chain=False):
    """Standard MSR session: prefix MRS/STR to DEST, arming dumps (DEST and the whole planted
    stream), MARK, the stimulus steps, then the result dump.
    #562: restore=True also plants TGT_OPS at TGT, sets lr=TGT and the LDMW word to TGT, and
    arms all three (a fall-through past the restore lands on b_self and reads `nofire').
    `regs' are debugger assignments made BEFORE the prefix; `poke' ones AFTER the arming dumps
    (a `cpsr=' poke must follow the prefix, whose MRS arms DEST == 0xd3).
    chain=True (with restore): TGT holds `movs pc,lr' and the readback TGT_OPS move to TGT2,
    where lr points -- so one session can run an LDM^ restore and then a MOVS restore."""
    full = PREFIX + ops
    cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
    extra = {}
    rb = TGT2 if chain else TGT
    if restore:
        cmds += plant(TGT_OPS, at=rb) + ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (LDMW, TGT),
                                         "lr=0x%x" % rb, "r5=0x%x" % LDMW]
        for i, k in enumerate(TGT_OPS + ["b_self"]):
            extra[rb + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
        extra[LDMW] = TGT
        if chain:
            cmds += plant(["movs_pc_lr"], at=TGT)
            extra[TGT] = WORDS["movs_pc_lr"]
            extra[TGT + 4] = WORDS["b_self"]
    cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
                          dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
    if restore:
        cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT, TGT + 4 * len(TGT_OPS)), dumpcmd(LDMW)]
        if chain:
            cmds += [dumpcmd(TGT2, TGT2 + 4 * len(TGT_OPS))]
    cmds += [MARK] + list(poke) + ["step %d" % s for s in steps_after] \
        + [dumpcmd(DEST, late_end)]
    return cmds, (full, extra)


def arming(early, full):
    """R4: DEST == 0xd3 after the prefix, and every planted word reads back as its WORDS entry.
    #562: `full' may be (ops, {addr: word}) -- the extra plants (restore target, LDM word)."""
    extra = {}
    if isinstance(full, tuple):
        full, extra = full
    if dumped(early, DEST) != BOOT_CPSR:
        return False
    for i, k in enumerate(full):
        if dumped(early, CODE + 4 * i) != WORDS[k]:
            return False
    for a, w in extra.items():
        if dumped(early, a) != w:
            return False
    return True


def prog_session(code_ops, blocks=(), data=None, regs=(), segments=((), ()), late_end=DEST + 12):
    """#562 pass 2: a multi-block program.  code_ops at CODE (after PREFIX), each (addr, ops) block
    at addr, each {addr: word} data word planted; every block and data word is ARMED (dumped and
    checked before MARK).  `segments' = ((pokes, steps), ...) after MARK: debugger assignments,
    then `step N'."""
    data = data or {}
    full = PREFIX + list(code_ops)
    cmds = prologue() + plant(full)
    extra = {}
    for a, ops in blocks:
        cmds += plant(ops, at=a)
        for i, k in enumerate(list(ops) + ["b_self"]):
            extra[a + 4 * i] = WORDS[k]
    for a, w in data.items():
        cmds.append("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (a, w))
        extra[a] = w
    cmds += list(regs) + ["pc=0x%x" % CODE, "step 2", dumpcmd(DEST),
                          dumpcmd(CODE, CODE + 4 * len(full))]
    for a, ops in blocks:
        cmds.append(dumpcmd(a, a + 4 * len(ops)))
    for a in data:
        cmds.append(dumpcmd(a))
    cmds.append(MARK)
    for pokes, steps in segments:
        cmds += list(pokes) + (["step %d" % steps] if steps else [])
    cmds.append(dumpcmd(DEST, late_end))
    early, late, alive, marked = _session_split(cmds)
    return early, late, alive, marked, (full, extra)


def announces(t, key):
    """#562 pass 2: how MANY `(once)' lines the named site printed -- a latch that never
    suppresses prints twice when its site is visited twice; any() could not see that."""
    return sum(1 for ln in t.splitlines() if key in ln and "(once)" in ln)


def diag_unimpl(nm, t):
    """#562: which register-bank `unimplemented mode' text a session printed (HEAD: the
    function that exit(1)ed -- the reason under test; fixed: the backstop that announced)."""
    hits = sorted(set(re.findall(r"arm_(save|load)_register_bank: unimplemented mode (\d+)", t)))
    diagnosis.append("%s: %s" % (nm, ", ".join("%s mode %s" % h for h in hits) if hits
                                 else "no bank text"))
